After analyzing the text, I found that the following Cloud Providers were impacted:

1. Microsoft Azure
2. Azur e SQL Datab ase 
3. Azur e SQL Data Warehouse/S ynapse Analy tics
4. Azur e Datab ase for MySQL
5. Azur e Datab ase for P ostgreSQL
6. Azur e Datab ase for MariaDB

Here is the breakdown of each outage per Cloud Provider:

**Microsoft Azure**

* Service: SQL Database, SQL Data Warehouse/Synapse Analytics, MySQL, PostgreSQL, MariaDB
* Region: Japan East
* Availability Zone: Not specified
* Impact Date: 2020-07-01 09:24 UTC
* Duration: 6 hours and 36 minutes
* Root Cause: Connections to Azure SQL Database and related data services went through a load-balanced set of front-end nodes (Gateways) that provided directory lookup services and rerouted incoming connections to the intended backend nodes hosting the database. One of the SQL Gateway clusters became unhealthy, causing intermittent impact on login availability.
* Downtime Period: 2020-07-01 09:24 UTC - 2020-07-02 03:40 UTC
* Resolved Date: 2020-07-02 03:40 UTC
